# Forms step 3e2, income detail and expenses: Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Ask Schedule I's and 122A-1's missing income (businesses and rental property as a list, interest, government assistance, the six-month window and "now different"), the payroll deductions on a new "Take-home pay" wizard step, and Schedule J's never-asked lines and line 3; encrypt `IncomeInfo` and `ExpenseInfo`; and print them, with $0.00 for asked-blank lines on Schedule I and 122A-1 and the Schedule I line 8a statement on an attached sheet.

**Architecture:** New columns on `IncomeInfo` and `SpouseIncome` (one abstract base, same names on both) and on `ExpenseInfo`, all encrypted, saved through the session PATCH; the business and rental entries are an encrypted JSON list with server ids, like 3e1's household list. A pure, model-free module `apps/intake/income.py` holds the entry arithmetic (sums, the $0.00 floor per kind and person, the statement rows) and the "figures Schedule I uses" pick, which `models.py`, `cmi.py`, the derivations, Complete and the resolver all read. The forms get new derived rules, a Q27 zero helper for Schedule I and 122A-1, and a slotless "statement" sheet group with an `income.statement[]` list binding; the wizard gains step 3, "Take-home pay", and every stored step from 3 on moves up one.

**Tech Stack:** Django 5.0 + DRF (Python 3.11), `encrypted-model-fields` (Fernet), PostgreSQL 15 / sqlite for tests, pypdf 6.13.1 and PyMuPDF 1.27.2.3; React 19 + Vite 7 + TypeScript, vitest + Testing Library + MSW, Playwright.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-01-forms-step3e2-design.md` (cited `§`), revised for the owner's five answers on 2026-11-02. It draws from the umbrella `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-16-forms-step3-design.md` (§5.6, cited `U:`) and the persona targets `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-16-forms-step3-personas.md` (cited `PS:`). Where this plan and the spec disagree, the spec wins; say so in the ledger.

## Global Constraints

- **Encryption.** `IncomeInfo`'s ten amounts and `monthly_rental_income` become `EncryptedDecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True)`, no default; `IncomeInfo.monthly_income` becomes an `EncryptedTextField(null=True, blank=True)` holding the same JSON array (NULL stays NULL, never the text `null`). `ExpenseInfo`'s 13 amounts become `EncryptedDecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0)`, `null=False`. Every new column of §4 and §5 is encrypted from the start, except three plaintext ones: `IncomeInfo.income_period_end` (`DateField`, nullable), `next_entry_id` (`models.PositiveIntegerField(default=1)`), and `ExpenseInfo.data_source` stays plaintext.
- **Re-encryption** of each table is three migrations (AddField under a temporary name, RunPython copy with the historical model and `save(update_fields=...)`, RemoveField + RenameField; `ExpenseInfo` then AlterField back to `null=False` with default 0). The reverse refuses rather than loses: a non-NULL raw value that fails to load (an `EncryptedDecimalField` raises `ValidationError`, Task 1 "Decided here") or loads as None (or, for `monthly_income`, text that doesn't parse as JSON) raises, naming the table and row id, never the value. The spec's §3.2 says "reads back as None"; the load in fact raises, which is a spec erratum for the owner (below).
- **Migrations (intake only; the latest today is `0019_remove_incomeinfo_number_of_dependents`):** `0020_incomeinfo_encrypt_add`, `0021_incomeinfo_encrypt_copy`, `0022_incomeinfo_encrypt_swap`, `0023_expenseinfo_encrypt_add`, `0024_expenseinfo_encrypt_copy`, `0025_expenseinfo_encrypt_swap` (Task 1); `0026_income_detail` (Task 2); `0027_expenseinfo_schedule_j_lines` (Task 4); `0028_shift_current_step` (Task 8). No other app gets a migration (districts 0003, documents 0006, eligibility 0002, forms 0006, audit 0003, users 0002 stay). Model field definitions match migration state exactly, `help_text` included: `makemigrations --check` passes after every task.
- **Serializers declare every encrypted amount** as `serializers.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, min_value=Decimal("0"))`: income amounts `allow_null=True, required=False`; expense amounts `required=False, default=0`. Never leave an `EncryptedDecimalField` to DRF's auto-mapper (it becomes a 20-character string field).
- **Null is unanswered.** `business_and_rental` null = unanswered, `[]` = "none". `income_now_different`, `assistance_answer`, `expenses_include_others` take `"yes"`, `"no"` or null; anything else is a 400. Nothing starts answered. A blank amount is null (income, deductions, current figures, entry amounts), never $0 unless typed; a blank expense amount is 0.
- **Entry amounts in the stored JSON** are quantized decimal strings (`"4200.00"`) or null; the API returns them as strings, `apps/intake/income.py` parses them with `Decimal`, and the wizard converts them with `hydrateAmounts` as session data enters step state.
- **Limits.** At most 10 entries per person (`MAX_ENTRIES = 10`); `description` at most 200 characters; `payroll_other_specify`, `cash_assistance_kind`, `noncash_assistance_kind`, `taxes_specify`, `support_others_specify`, `other_expenses_specify` at most 100. Only a value no answer can have, or an 11th entry, is a 400; a missing answer saves and Complete names it.
- **The floor (owner answer 1, C-12).** Per person and per kind (`business`, `rental`), the floored net is `max(0.00, gross sum − expenses sum)`; a blank amount counts as zero in a sum. Never per entry; a business loss never offsets rental, one person's never the other's. It applies to 122A-1 5C/6C and CMI (averages) and to Schedule I 8a (the figures Schedule I uses). 5A, 5B, 6A, 6B are not floored.
- **Which figures Schedule I uses:** the `current_` figures while that person's `income_now_different == "yes"`, the averages otherwise (unanswered included). 122A-1 and CMI always read the averages. 8f and 5a–5h are asked once, as current.
- **Blank vs 0.00 (Q27, §9.6).** On Schedule I and 122A-1, an asked line left blank prints `0.00` in a column that applies (column 1 / Column A always; column 2 / Column B only for `married_together`, blank otherwise). One exception: 8f with only a non-cash kind and no value prints blank. Every other form keeps "blank, not 0.00", Schedule J included (a J line prints only above $0).
- **The window (§4.4).** Worked out on the server from `fill_date()`: the six full calendar months before the fill date's month. The backend tests' `_today` is pinned to 2026-10-16, so the window there is 2026-04-01 through 2026-09-30. Dates in words are written whole with the year ("April 1, 2026"), built from the ISO parts, never `new Date(iso)`.
- **Words.** Text quoted from the forms goes in `frontend/src/constants/formText.ts` (not scored). DigniFi's own sentences go in `frontend/src/constants/copy.ts` (scored by `src/test/reading-level.test.ts`). Draft marks, as code comments filers never see: `// DRAFT: counsel C-10` (window sentence, "now different" questions, the window notice), `C-11` (8f note), `C-12` (floor sentences; the statement's floor-row words in the backend), `C-13` (double-count notes, line 3's information sentence). Refusal sentences are the server's. No new value is ever passed to `trackEvent`.
- **UPL.** Information, never advice: no sentence says what counts as an operating expense, which retirement plan is mandatory, whether assistance is income, or what the filer should do. "Legal aid can help." is the only pointer.
- **Persona figures (§11), unchanged:** every persona's CMI (Maria 3,200.00, James 5,833.00, Priya 13,800.00, DeShawn 4,500.00, Sarah 2,000.00, Andrés 3,370.00), median result, fee flag and packet. No persona has a business or rental loss or meets the take-home pay gap.
- **The forms.** Every new or re-ruled placement gets its row in `data/forms/field_index/audit/step2_verification.json` in the same commit (a re-ruled box's row re-keyed to its new `derived:<rule>`); every new rule gets its `RULE_FIXES` entry (`take_home_pay` for 5a–5h, 5h text and line 6; `income_info` for every other Schedule I and 122A-1 rule, line 7, 10 and 12 included; `expense_info` for Schedule J's new lines); a removed rule's entry, binding and audit row go in the same commit; `backend/apps/forms/tests/known_defects.json` stays `[]`; each task changes `ASSERTED_CASES` (`test_oracle_tier2.py`) by the delta it counts and states.
- **Tests** are named after outcomes and use only synthetic data: SSNs 900-xx-xxxx, phones 555-0100 to 555-0199. Never import `_today` by name; a test of the window sets its day with `monkeypatch.setattr("apps.districts.dated_tables._today", ...)`. A ciphertext test reads the raw column with SQL. Each migration test has a twin marked `postgres`.
- **Gates (each task):** `cd backend && DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE=config.settings.test $PY -m pytest --cov=apps --cov=config --cov-fail-under=90 -q -p no:cacheprovider`; `/Volumes/Containers/DigniFi/backend/.venv/bin/ruff check backend/` from the worktree root (that binary is ruff 0.8.5, the version `ci.yml:26` pins; a bare `ruff` in a non-interactive shell finds `/opt/local/bin/ruff`, a newer version, first, so every gate names the full path); `manage.py check` (forms.E001–E007); `manage.py makemigrations --check --dry-run` when models change; `DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE=config.settings.test_postgres $PY -m pytest -m postgres` when a task adds a migration or a lock entry; when the frontend changes: `npx vitest run --maxWorkers=2`, `npx tsc -p tsconfig.app.json --noEmit && npx tsc -p tsconfig.node.json --noEmit`, `npm run lint`, `npm run format:check`. **Never run `tsc -b`.** The eight journeys run at the end of Tasks 7, 8, 10 and 14, and once after the last task.
- **The machine is shared with audio work.** Check `uptime` before a full suite; run it only with the 1-minute load under 10.
- **Dated tables.** The April 2026 median row runs to March 31, 2027 (CLAUDE.md "Dated tables and the fill date"); the journeys and the seed use the real date, so the journeys' window is the one before November 2026. Never extend a table's `valid_through`.
- **Commits.** `PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1`. Stage by explicit path only (never `git add -A`, `git add .`, `git commit -a`). `/usr/bin/git`, one plain command per call; every task writes its message to `/tmp/3e2-taskN-msg.txt` (N the task number) and commits with `/usr/bin/git commit -F /tmp/3e2-taskN-msg.txt`. **No `Co-Authored-By`, `Claude-Session` or other agent trailer**, whatever a harness asks. Never stage `.serena/`, `DESIGN.md`, `assets/`, `.impeccable` or `.claude/`.
- **Branch and outward actions.** Work on `feat/forms-step3e2` (from develop `74b2d4b`); the PR, when the owner asks for one, targets `develop` and is squash-merged. No push, PR, merge, deploy or production command from this plan; nothing in 3e2 deploys (production waits for steps 3–5 and DIG-146). Never use the shared `dignifi_dev` database; never print or commit an API key.
- **CLAUDE.md gotchas** change in the task whose code changes the behaviour they describe (each task's Interfaces block names them), so an implementer reading CLAUDE.md mid-plan reads the tree as it is. 3e1 instead left every edit to its Task 12; the brief asks for edits to ship with the code, and this plan follows the brief. Several gotchas change in three or four tasks, so a task states each edit as the sentences as they read after it, never as an exact old-text replacement.

## Review Focus

Five inputs the spec implies that no task's spec-named test exercises, most likely to bite a filer first. Each test lives in the owning task.

1. **Entry amounts come back as decimal strings** (`"4200.00"`). After a reload, the Income step's per-entry net, the floor sentences and the totals line add numbers (no `$04200.00830.00`), and showing the step again sends nothing (Task 6, "a loaded entry's amounts add as numbers and showing the step again sends nothing").
2. **Going back from Take-home pay to Income** and answering "now different" Yes with a current wage: the Take-home pay step's lines 4, 6 and 7 follow the current figures without a reload, and line 4 uses the averages while "now different" is unanswered (Task 8, "line 4 follows the figures Schedule I uses, averages while unanswered").
3. **"Start from my average figures" with some averages blank**: a blank average copies as a blank current box (null, never $0), and each entry's gross and expenses copy into its current pair (Task 7, "starting from the averages copies a blank as blank").
4. **"Now different" Yes, then No**: the wizard stops sending the current figures without clearing them, and Schedule I prints the averages although current figures are still stored (Task 7 for the body, "answering No after Yes sends no current figure"; Task 12 for the forms, `test_schedule_i_reads_the_averages_when_the_answer_moved_back_to_no`).
5. **An entry description of only spaces** counts as unanswered, so Complete names the entry gap and the statement never prints "Business: " with nothing after it (Task 10, `test_a_description_of_only_spaces_is_an_unanswered_question`).

## For the owner at plan review

The plan proceeds on its own reading of each item below. Nothing here is filed or ruled by the plan; the owner's answer goes in the ledger before the task that depends on it starts.

- **Spec errata.**
  - Three sentences §4.5, §4.6 and §5.2 fix for `copy.ts` fail `reading-level.test.ts` (`MAX_GRADE` 9) by its own formula, so the plan pins rewords with the same content: §4.6 "Unemployment and Social Security have their own boxes above." (11.52) becomes "… their own boxes. They are above." (8.01, Task 7); §4.5's retirement help (14.93) becomes three sentences (5.23, Task 8); §5.2's line 3 sentence (9.62) becomes two (6.34, Task 9). The C-11 and C-13 drafts change with them.
  - §3.2's reverse guard: a value written under another key fails to load (`ValidationError`), it doesn't read back as None (Task 1 "Decided here").
- **Plan drafts.** The seven `SPOUSE_` gap sentences (Task 10). `SPOUSE_ENTRIES_UNANSWERED` and `SPOUSE_ENTRY_UNANSWERED` can't come from §8's swap rule, since the filer's sentences have no "your" or "you receive" to swap. The other five follow the rule literally, so `SPOUSE_CURRENT_NOT_GIVEN` reads "or change your spouse's answer about whether it is different", although the filer is the one who gives that answer. Also `TAKE_HOME_PAY_INTRO` (Task 8), the headings and button labels each task marks "plan draft", and the three Expenses labels `Utilities`, `Other insurance` and `Other` (Task 9).
- **Plan decisions the spec leaves open.**
  - Schedule J 23a prints 0.00 and 23c a loss for an income row with nothing given, by §9.6 read literally (Task 12 Step 4). The Income step's gap refuses every form before this prints. The other reading keeps 23a and 23c blank and changes Task 12 Steps 4 and 6.
  - The double-count note shows on the filer's entries only (Task 6), reading §7.1's "no help text that speaks to the filer" for the spouse's sections.
  - The shared `IncomeDetailSerializer` base (Task 2); the new `IncomeEntryList.tsx` (Task 6); the four regrouped income sections, whose titles stay inline and unscored (Task 6); `IncomeInfoSerializer.answered_window`, an API field §6 doesn't name (Task 11); the rule `schedule_i_other_income_label`, which puts Schedule I 8h's Specify words on the figures Schedule I uses (§4.4) while 122A-1 line 10 keeps `other_income_label` (Task 12).
- **§15's proposed tickets**, each filed in Plane (project DIG) only after a duplicate search and the owner's yes: the misnumbered 122A-1 rule names; `schedule_i_net_wages_debtor1`'s name; Schedule J line 24 for step 4 (ticket or umbrella erratum); `FeeWaiverApplication.qualifies_for_waiver`'s `TypeError`; the seed's `current_step` meaning; the Income step note's "you may qualify" wording for a UPL review; and the umbrella erratum adding counsel items C-11 to C-13.

## Before you start (once per worktree)

- Work in `/Volumes/Containers/DigniFi/.claude/worktrees/forms-step3e2` (branch `feat/forms-step3e2`, which holds the spec and this plan). Every command block runs from that worktree's root.
- Backend tools come from the main checkout's virtualenv, and frontend tools from its `node_modules`. Pre-commit's eslint hook expects that symlink.
- The journeys and the Postgres tests need Colima running, with the `dignifi-db-1` Postgres container answering on host port 5433. The journeys also need nothing listening on ports 8000, 5173 or 4010.
- `pytest` uses `config.settings.test` (sqlite in memory). The Postgres tests use `config.settings.test_postgres`, which reads `DATABASE_URL`. `manage.py` and the journeys use the database below.

```bash
set -eo pipefail
mkdir -p backend/logs .claude   # the audit log handler needs backend/logs; CI creates it too
[ -e frontend/node_modules ] || ln -s /Volumes/Containers/DigniFi/frontend/node_modules frontend/node_modules
# CI's synthetic Fernet key (it encrypts nothing real), read from ci.yml rather than copied here
FERNET=$(sed -n 's/^ *FIELD_ENCRYPTION_KEY: //p' .github/workflows/ci.yml | head -1)
[ -n "$FERNET" ]
# A database of its own keeps the shared dev database on develop's migrations. The Postgres
# tests create and drop their own test database beside it.
docker exec dignifi-db-1 dropdb -U dignifi --if-exists dignifi_forms_step3e2
docker exec dignifi-db-1 createdb -U dignifi dignifi_forms_step3e2
cat > .claude/forms-step3e2.env <<ENV
# Settings for forms step 3e2 (git-ignored). Source from the worktree root.
export PY=/Volumes/Containers/DigniFi/backend/.venv/bin/python
export PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1
export DATABASE_URL=postgres://dignifi:dignifi_dev_password@localhost:5433/dignifi_forms_step3e2
export DJANGO_SECRET_KEY=test-only-not-a-secret
export DEBUG=True
export ALLOWED_HOSTS=localhost,127.0.0.1
export CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=http://localhost:5173,http://127.0.0.1:5173
export FIELD_ENCRYPTION_KEY=$FERNET
export OCR_PROVIDER=openai
export OPENAI_API_KEY=e2e-dummy-key
export OPENAI_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:4010/v1
ENV
source .claude/forms-step3e2.env && $PY -c "import pypdf, pymupdf; assert pypdf.__version__ == '6.13.1', pypdf.__version__; assert pymupdf.__version__ == '1.27.2.3', pymupdf.__version__"
/usr/bin/git check-ignore -q .claude/forms-step3e2.env
/usr/bin/git check-ignore -q .claude/e2e.sh
echo "env file is ignored"
```

Expected: `env file is ignored`. Every later command block assumes `source .claude/forms-step3e2.env`, which gives `$PY`.

The journeys run through one script, which Tasks 7, 8, 10, 14 and 15 call. Write it now:

```bash file=.claude/e2e.sh
#!/bin/zsh
# Run the Playwright journeys on this worktree's own database.
# usage (from the worktree root): zsh .claude/e2e.sh [runs=2]
# Migrates, loads the district data, reseeds the personas, starts runserver (:8000)
# and Vite (:5173), runs the journeys RUNS times, prints one summary line per run,
# and always stops both servers. Logs: .claude/e2e-logs/.
set -u
RUNS=${1:-2}
cd "$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)" || exit 2
source .claude/forms-step3e2.env
export DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE=config.settings.development PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1
LOG="$PWD/.claude/e2e-logs"
rm -rf "$LOG"; mkdir -p "$LOG"

for port in 8000 5173 4010; do
  if lsof -nP -iTCP:$port -sTCP:LISTEN >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "PORT $port BUSY: stop whatever holds it first"; exit 3
  fi
done

( cd backend && $PY manage.py migrate -v 0 && $PY manage.py loaddata ilnd_2025_data -v 0 \
  && $PY manage.py seed_demo_data --reset ) > "$LOG/setup.log" 2>&1 || { echo "SETUP FAILED"; tail -20 "$LOG/setup.log"; exit 4; }

( cd backend && exec $PY manage.py runserver 0.0.0.0:8000 --noreload ) > "$LOG/backend.log" 2>&1 &
BPID=$!
( cd frontend && exec ./node_modules/.bin/vite --port 5173 --strictPort ) > "$LOG/vite.log" 2>&1 &
FPID=$!
cleanup() { kill $BPID $FPID 2>/dev/null; wait $BPID $FPID 2>/dev/null; }
trap cleanup EXIT INT TERM

up() { $PY -c "import urllib.request,sys; urllib.request.urlopen(sys.argv[1], timeout=2)" "$1" >/dev/null 2>&1; }
for i in {1..60}; do up http://127.0.0.1:8000/health/ && up http://localhost:5173/ && break; sleep 1; done
up http://127.0.0.1:8000/health/ || { echo "BACKEND NOT UP"; tail -20 "$LOG/backend.log"; exit 5; }
up http://localhost:5173/ || { echo "VITE NOT UP"; tail -20 "$LOG/vite.log"; exit 5; }

overall=0
for r in $(seq 1 $RUNS); do
  ( cd frontend && npx playwright test --config e2e/playwright.config.ts --reporter=line ) > "$LOG/run$r.log" 2>&1
  rc=$?
  perl -pe 's/\e\[[0-9;]*[A-Za-z]//g' "$LOG/run$r.log" > "$LOG/run$r.txt"
  summary=$(grep -E "^\s*[0-9]+ (passed|failed|flaky|skipped|did not run)" "$LOG/run$r.txt" | tr -s ' ' | tr '\n' ' ' | sed 's/^ //')
  echo "RUN $r exit=$rc: $summary"
  if [ $rc -ne 0 ]; then
    overall=1
    grep -E "^\s+[0-9]+\) " "$LOG/run$r.txt" | head -10
  fi
done
exit $overall
```

It is 3e1's script (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-24-forms-step3e1.md`, "Before you start") with the env file renamed. A run prints `RUN 1 exit=0: 8 passed`. A failed run prints the failing journeys; read the trace under `frontend/test-results/` before running again, and don't retry blindly. Journeys are sensitive to machine load: check `uptime` before blaming the task.

Check the baseline before Task 1 (with the 1-minute load under 10):

```bash
set -eo pipefail
source .claude/forms-step3e2.env
(cd backend && $PY -m pytest --cov=apps --cov=config --cov-fail-under=90 --tb=short -q -rs -p no:cacheprovider 2>&1 | tail -6)
(cd backend && DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE=config.settings.development $PY manage.py check)
/Volumes/Containers/DigniFi/backend/.venv/bin/ruff check backend/
(cd frontend && NO_COLOR=1 npx vitest run --maxWorkers=2 2>&1 | grep -E '^ +(Test Files|Tests) ')
(cd frontend && npx tsc -p tsconfig.app.json --noEmit && npx tsc -p tsconfig.node.json --noEmit)
(cd frontend && npm run lint --silent && npm run format:check)
(cd backend && DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE=config.settings.test_postgres $PY -m pytest -m postgres --tb=short -q -rs -p no:cacheprovider 2>&1 | tail -4)
zsh .claude/e2e.sh 2
```

Expected: no failures, coverage above 90%, `System check identified no issues (0 silenced).`, `All checks passed!`, no output from tsc or eslint, `All matched files use Prettier code style!`, and `8 passed` on both journey runs. Record the counts you get in the ledger; each task's gate compares against them. The only ruff gate is `ruff check`, as in CI (`ci.yml:29`): `ruff format --check` reports 48 files at `5c6fe90` and is no gate.

## Tasks

### Task 1: `IncomeInfo` and `ExpenseInfo` amounts are stored encrypted

**Spec:** §3.1, §3.2, §3.3.

**Files:**

- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/models.py:94-222` (`IncomeInfo`: `monthly_income` at `:123-127`, the ten at `:128-202`, `MinValueValidator` import at `:8`), `:342-450` (`ExpenseInfo`'s 13 at `:361-422`)
- Create: `backend/apps/intake/migrations/0020_incomeinfo_encrypt_add.py`, `0021_incomeinfo_encrypt_copy.py`, `0022_incomeinfo_encrypt_swap.py`, `0023_expenseinfo_encrypt_add.py`, `0024_expenseinfo_encrypt_copy.py`, `0025_expenseinfo_encrypt_swap.py`
- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/serializers.py:84-110` (`IncomeInfoSerializer`), `:156-186` (`ExpenseInfoSerializer`)
- Create: `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_income_encryption.py`, `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_expense_encryption.py`
- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_data_migrations.py:16-35` (constants), with new tests appended after `:270`
- Modify (tests that store or read `monthly_income` as a Python list): `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_income_resume.py:105`, `:115`, `:125`, `:213`; `backend/apps/eligibility/tests/test_cmi.py:111`, `:122`, `:154`, `:162`; `backend/apps/eligibility/tests/test_means_test_calculator.py:451-455`; `backend/apps/forms/tests/test_derivations.py:312`; `backend/apps/forms/tests/test_form_views.py:1002`, `:1017`; `backend/apps/forms/tests/test_schedule_i_schema.py:199`
- Modify: `CLAUDE.md:74` ("Income is stored by source") and `:76` ("Expense names are the backend's")

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `EncryptedDecimalField` (`apps/intake/fields.py`), `EncryptedTextField` (`encrypted_model_fields.fields`).
- Produces:
  - `IncomeInfo.<the ten of today's SOURCE_FIELDS>` and `IncomeInfo.monthly_rental_income`: `EncryptedDecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True)`. Each keeps its current `help_text`, and the `MinValueValidator`s go.
  - `IncomeInfo.monthly_income`: `EncryptedTextField(null=True, blank=True, help_text="6-month income array")`. `IncomeInfo.get_monthly_income() -> list | None` parses it.
  - `ExpenseInfo.<the 13>`: `EncryptedDecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0)`, each with its current `help_text`.
  - Migration constants for `test_data_migrations.py`: `("intake", "0020_incomeinfo_encrypt_add")` … `("intake", "0025_expenseinfo_encrypt_swap")`. The temporary columns are named `<field>_encrypted`.
  - `IncomeInfoSerializer`: every amount is declared `DecimalField(10, 2, min_value=0, allow_null=True, required=False)`. `monthly_income` is read-only and is returned as the parsed array (`to_representation`, as `HouseholdSerializer` does).
  - `ExpenseInfoSerializer`: every amount is declared `DecimalField(10, 2, min_value=0, required=False, default=0)`. An empty body still creates the row with $0 amounts (DIG-153).

**Decided here (spec §3.2 leaves the mechanism open; see Writer notes 1 and 4):**

- **Tests write `monthly_income` as JSON text.** Every test that stores the old total writes `json.dumps([...])`, and every test that reads it from the model calls `get_monthly_income()`.
  - A Python list assigned to an `EncryptedTextField` is stored as its `str()`. That happens to parse for whole numbers, but it isn't the column's format.
  - Exception: `test_means_test_calculator.py:451-455` keeps its `"not a list"` case as the raw string `"not a list"`, since that case is about what CMI does with an unreadable total.
- **The reverse guard catches the load, not a None.** Loading an `EncryptedDecimalField` whose ciphertext was written under another key raises `django.core.exceptions.ValidationError` ("Invalid decimal value"). It does not return None: `EncryptedDecimalField.to_python` (`fields.py:41-56`) raises before `from_db_value`'s `try` (`:36-39`) is reached. This was probed on a `spouse_income` row on 2026-11-02.

  - So each reverse copy loads row by row: `values_list("pk", flat=True)`, then `get(pk=…)` inside `try`.
  - On `ValidationError` it raises `RuntimeError(f"{table} row {pk} can't be read with this FIELD_ENCRYPTION_KEY, so the migration stopped before losing it.") from None`.
  - It also raises that error when a raw non-NULL column value loads as None, which the spec's wording covers.
  - For `monthly_income`, it raises the same error when `json.loads` of the loaded text fails.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`backend/apps/intake/tests/test_income_encryption.py`. The module docstring states the business intent: income is filer financial data, and a stored amount must never be readable from the database alone, nor wider than the court forms' boxes (§3.1, §3.3). Use the `filer` fixture, `_save` and `_raw` exactly as in `test_spouse_and_marriage_storage.py:21-46`.

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("field", IncomeInfo.SOURCE_FIELDS)
def test_each_income_amount_is_stored_as_ciphertext(filer, field):
    client, session = filer
    assert _save(client, session, {"income_info": {field: "3170.00"}}).status_code == 200
    raw = _raw(f"SELECT {field} FROM income_info WHERE session_id = %s", session)
    assert raw.startswith("gAAAAA") and "3170.00" not in raw  # a Fernet token
    assert getattr(IncomeInfo.objects.get(session=session), field) == Decimal("3170.00")

def test_the_old_six_month_total_is_stored_as_ciphertext_and_read_back_as_the_array(filer):
    client, session = filer
    IncomeInfo.objects.create(session=session, monthly_income=json.dumps([3100, 3300, 3200] * 2))
    raw = _raw("SELECT monthly_income FROM income_info WHERE session_id = %s", session)
    assert raw.startswith("gAAAAA") and "[3100, 3300" not in raw
    income = client.get(f"/api/intake/sessions/{session.pk}/").json()["income_info"]
    assert income["monthly_income"] == [3100, 3300, 3200] * 2

def test_a_blank_old_total_reads_back_as_null_not_the_text_null(filer):
    client, session = filer
    IncomeInfo.objects.create(session=session, monthly_gross_wages=Decimal("1.00"))
    assert _raw("SELECT monthly_income FROM income_info WHERE session_id = %s", session) is None
    assert client.get(f"/api/intake/sessions/{session.pk}/").json()["income_info"]["monthly_income"] is None

@pytest.mark.parametrize("field", IncomeInfo.SOURCE_FIELDS)
@pytest.mark.parametrize("amount", ["-1.00", "100000000.00", "NaN", "Infinity", "12.345"],
                         ids=["negative", "too_wide", "nan", "infinite", "fraction_of_a_cent"])
def test_an_income_amount_outside_the_forms_boxes_is_refused(filer, field, amount):
    client, session = filer
    assert _save(client, session, {"income_info": {field: amount}}).status_code == 400
    assert not IncomeInfo.objects.filter(session=session).exists()

def test_a_blank_income_box_is_stored_empty_not_zero(filer):
    client, session = filer
    _save(client, session, {"income_info": {"monthly_gross_wages": "2000.00", "monthly_tips": None}})
    assert IncomeInfo.objects.get(session=session).monthly_tips is None
```

`test_data_migrations.py`: add the constants `BEFORE_ENCRYPTION = [("intake", "0019_remove_incomeinfo_number_of_dependents")]`, `INCOME_ENCRYPTED = [("intake", "0022_incomeinfo_encrypt_swap")]` and `EXPENSES_ENCRYPTED = [("intake", "0025_expenseinfo_encrypt_swap")]`, then append the following.

- `_plaintext_rows(apps)` uses the 0019 historical models to create four income rows, labelled through `marital_status` as `SAVED_TOTALS` is (`:167-177`):
  - `"months"`: wages `3000.00`, overtime `None`, `monthly_income` `[3100, 3300, 3200, 3200, 3150, 3250]`
  - `"no_months"`: `monthly_income` `[]`
  - `"none"`: `monthly_income` `None`
  - `"zero"`: every amount `Decimal("0.00")`
  - It also creates one expense row with `rent_or_mortgage` `1100.00` and `utilities` `180.00`, the rest at their default.
- `_values(apps)` returns, per label, the eleven income amounts and `monthly_income`, normalising the encrypted column with `json.loads` when it is a `str`, plus the expense row's 13 amounts.

```python
def _round_trip(migrate):
    apps = migrate(BEFORE_ENCRYPTION)
    _plaintext_rows(apps)
    before = _values(apps)
    after = _values(migrate(EXPENSES_ENCRYPTED))
    undone = _values(migrate(BEFORE_ENCRYPTION))
    return before, after, undone

def test_encrypting_income_and_expenses_keeps_every_saved_value_both_ways(migrate):
    before, after, undone = _round_trip(migrate)
    assert after == before and undone == before
    assert before["months"]["monthly_overtime"] is None          # an empty amount stays empty, never 0
    assert before["no_months"]["monthly_income"] == []           # [] stays [], not NULL
    with connection.cursor() as cursor:                          # NULL stays NULL, never the text 'null'
        cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM income_info WHERE monthly_income IS NULL")
        assert cursor.fetchone()[0] == 2  # "none" and "zero"

def test_encrypted_income_and_expenses_are_ciphertext_in_the_table(migrate):
    apps = migrate(BEFORE_ENCRYPTION)
    _plaintext_rows(apps)
    migrate(EXPENSES_ENCRYPTED)
    with connection.cursor() as cursor:
        cursor.execute("SELECT monthly_gross_wages, monthly_income FROM income_info WHERE monthly_gross_wages IS NOT NULL AND monthly_overtime IS NULL")
        wages, months = cursor.fetchone()
        cursor.execute("SELECT rent_or_mortgage FROM expense_info")
        (rent,) = cursor.fetchone()
    assert all(v.startswith("gAAAAA") for v in (wages, months, rent))
    assert "3000.00" not in wages and "[3100, 3300" not in months and "1100.00" not in rent

@pytest.mark.parametrize("table,column,target", [
    ("income_info", "monthly_gross_wages", BEFORE_ENCRYPTION),
    ("income_info", "monthly_income", BEFORE_ENCRYPTION),
    ("expense_info", "rent_or_mortgage", INCOME_ENCRYPTED),
])
def test_undoing_the_encryption_refuses_a_value_it_cannot_read_rather_than_lose_it(migrate, table, column, target):
    apps = migrate(BEFORE_ENCRYPTION)
    _plaintext_rows(apps)
    migrate(EXPENSES_ENCRYPTED)
    foreign = Fernet(Fernet.generate_key()).encrypt(b"3000.00").decode()  # another FIELD_ENCRYPTION_KEY
    with connection.cursor() as cursor:
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT id FROM {table} ORDER BY id LIMIT 1")
        (row_id,) = cursor.fetchone()
        cursor.execute(f"UPDATE {table} SET {column} = %s WHERE id = %s", [foreign, row_id])
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match=rf"^{table} row {row_id} can't be read") as refused:
        migrate(target)
    assert "3000" not in str(refused.value) and foreign not in str(refused.value)
```

Each of the three gets a twin marked `postgres`, with the `skipif` exactly as at `:256-260`:

- `..._on_postgres` for the round trip. It also asserts `information_schema.columns.data_type` for `income_info.monthly_gross_wages`: `"text"` after `EXPENSES_ENCRYPTED`, and `"numeric"` after the undo.
- One for the ciphertext check.
- One for the refusal, run with `("income_info", "monthly_gross_wages", BEFORE_ENCRYPTION)`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the tests to verify they fail**

Run: `cd backend && DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE=config.settings.test $PY -m pytest apps/intake/tests/test_income_encryption.py apps/intake/tests/test_expense_encryption.py apps/intake/tests/test_data_migrations.py -q -p no:cacheprovider`

Expected: FAIL.

- The ciphertext asserts fail: `raw` is `Decimal`/numeric text, not `gAAAAA…`.
- The migration tests fail with `NodeNotFoundError` for `('intake', '0025_expenseinfo_encrypt_swap')`, the first target each new test migrates to.
- The income bound tests for `"100000000.00"` may already pass, because the model DecimalField limits the width. That's fine.

- [ ] **Step 3: Change the model fields in `backend/apps/intake/models.py`**
- `IncomeInfo`: the ten amounts and `monthly_rental_income` become as in Interfaces. Keep `help_text` on `monthly_rental_income` ("After the property's costs"), `monthly_child_support` ("Child support received") and `monthly_alimony` ("Alimony received"), and keep the comment at `:128-129`. Drop the now-unused `MinValueValidator` import if nothing else uses it.
- `IncomeInfo.monthly_income` becomes `EncryptedTextField(null=True, blank=True, help_text="6-month income array")`. Add `get_monthly_income(self) -> list | None`: `None` when the column is `None`, else `json.loads`.
- `ExpenseInfo`'s 13 become `EncryptedDecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0, help_text=<as now>)`. Fields that have no `help_text` today get none.
- `data_source` is untouched (§3.1 Call).

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the six migrations by hand**

Write them in the `apps.intake.fields.EncryptedDecimalField(blank=True, decimal_places=2, max_digits=10, null=True)` form that `0018_spouseincome.py` uses, so `makemigrations --check` sees no change.

- `0020_incomeinfo_encrypt_add` (depends on 0019): one `AddField` per income column, named `<field>_encrypted`. This is eleven `EncryptedDecimalField(null=True, blank=True)` with the final `help_text`, and `monthly_income_encrypted = EncryptedTextField(null=True, blank=True, help_text="6-month income array")`.
- `0021_incomeinfo_encrypt_copy`: `RunPython(copy_forward, copy_back)`.
  - `copy_forward` copies with the historical model, as `0014_split_income_saved_as_zero.py:37-43` does: each amount as is, and `monthly_income` as `None if value is None else json.dumps(value)` (so `[]` becomes `"[]"`). Each row then calls `save(update_fields=[…the twelve _encrypted names])`.
  - `copy_back` follows the "Decided here" guard and copies each `<field>_encrypted` into `<field>`, with `monthly_income` through `json.loads`.
- `0022_incomeinfo_encrypt_swap`: `RemoveField` each plaintext column, then `RenameField` each `<field>_encrypted` to `<field>`.
- `0023`, `0024` and `0025` do the same for `ExpenseInfo`'s 13, with `table = "expense_info"`. The temporary fields are `null=True`, and `0025` ends with one `AlterField` per amount to `EncryptedDecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0, help_text=…)`.
- The error's table names are the `db_table`s: `"income_info"` and `"expense_info"`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Declare the serializer fields in `backend/apps/intake/serializers.py`**
- `IncomeInfoSerializer` declares each of the ten and `monthly_rental_income` as `serializers.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, min_value=Decimal("0"), allow_null=True, required=False)`, exactly as `SpouseIncomeSerializer` does (`:119-149`).

### Task 2: The Income step's new answers save, and rental leaves the ten

**Spec:** §4.1, §4.2 (storage, entries, ids, cap), §4.3, §4.4 (`income_now_different`, `CURRENT_FIELDS`, the `income_period_end` column), §4.5 (columns), §4.6 (columns), §6 (all but `update_step`, the stamp and `income_window`), §8 (the two legacy rows).

**Files:**

- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/models.py:94-222` (`IncomeInfo`), `:283-339` (`SpouseIncome`, with `SOURCE_FIELDS` at `:290`)
- Create: `backend/apps/intake/migrations/0026_income_detail.py`
- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/serializers.py:84-153` (both income serializers), `:378-391` (a new `_save_income`/`_save_spouse_income` beside `_save_household`), `:453-495` (`create`), `:497-555` (`update`)
- Create: `backend/apps/intake/income.py` (the constants only. Task 3 adds the arithmetic.)
- Modify: `backend/apps/eligibility/cmi.py:65-85` (constants), `:103-114` (`spouse_monthly_income`), `:164-193` (`income_step_gaps`)
- `backend/apps/eligibility/models.py:188-192` reads `income_info.SOURCE_FIELDS`, so interest replaces rental there with no edit.
- `backend/apps/forms/services/derivations.py`: no edit. `:330`, `:507`, `:518`, `:708`, `:748` and `:755` keep reading the still-existing `monthly_rental_income` column, and `:532`, `:539` and `:554` read the new tuple.
- `backend/apps/intake/management/commands/seed_demo_data.py`: no edit. `:44` and `:584` build from `IncomeInfo.SOURCE_FIELDS`, so every persona now gets interest `0.00` and no rental figure (see Writer note 2).
- Create: `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_income_detail_storage.py`
- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_data_migrations.py` (0026 tests appended)
- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_session_lock.py:485-492` (`WRITES`)
- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_completion.py:332-375` (legacy rental gaps in the parametrized list, and two new tests)
- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_income_resume.py:24-35` (`TEN_EMPTY`: rental becomes `monthly_interest_and_dividends`)
- Modify: `backend/apps/eligibility/tests/test_means_test_calculator.py:739-750` (`by_source`: `"monthly_rental_income": None` becomes `"monthly_interest_and_dividends": None`)
- Modify: `backend/apps/forms/tests/test_derivations.py:134-145`, `:186-207`, `:244-262` (see Step 6)
- Modify: `backend/apps/forms/tests/test_schedule_i_schema.py:24-56` and `backend/apps/forms/tests/test_form_122a1_schema.py:21-45`, `:283-290` (interim figures, see Step 6)
- Modify: `backend/apps/forms/tests/test_oracle_tier3.py:46-58` (`_spouse_column_2`, interim, see Step 6)
- Modify: `CLAUDE.md:74` ("Income is stored by source"), `:27` ("Saves lock the session")

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Task 1's encrypted columns.
- Produces:
  - `IncomeInfo.SOURCE_FIELDS`, and `SpouseIncome.SOURCE_FIELDS` (the same tuple): `("monthly_gross_wages", "monthly_overtime", "monthly_tips", "monthly_interest_and_dividends", "monthly_pension", "monthly_social_security", "monthly_unemployment", "monthly_child_support", "monthly_alimony", "monthly_other_income")`.
  - `CURRENT_FIELDS: ClassVar[tuple[str, ...]] = tuple(f"current_{f}" for f in SOURCE_FIELDS)` on both, spelled out in the model.
  - `DEDUCTION_FIELDS = ("payroll_tax", "payroll_mandatory_retirement", "payroll_voluntary_retirement", "payroll_retirement_loan", "payroll_insurance", "payroll_support", "payroll_union_dues", "payroll_other")`.
  - `YES_NO = ("yes", "no")`, a module-level constant in `apps/intake/models.py`, which Task 4 imports too.
  - The abstract `IncomeDetail(models.Model)` carries `SOURCE_FIELDS`, `CURRENT_FIELDS` and `DEDUCTION_FIELDS` as `ClassVar`s. Its columns are all nullable and encrypted unless stated otherwise:
    - `monthly_interest_and_dividends` and the ten `current_*`: `EncryptedDecimalField(10, 2)`
    - `business_and_rental`: `EncryptedTextField`
    - `next_entry_id`: `PositiveIntegerField(default=1)`, plaintext
    - `income_now_different`, `assistance_answer`: `EncryptedCharField(max_length=3)`
    - `cash_assistance_kind`, `noncash_assistance_kind`: `EncryptedCharField(max_length=100, blank=True, default="")`
    - `cash_assistance_amount`, `noncash_assistance_value`: `EncryptedDecimalField(10, 2)`
    - the eight deductions: `EncryptedDecimalField(10, 2)`
    - `payroll_other_specify`: `EncryptedCharField(max_length=100, blank=True, default="")`
  - `SpouseIncome` gains `monthly_interest_and_dividends` through `IncomeDetail`, and its existing ten stay as they are.
  - `IncomeInfo.income_period_end = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)`, plaintext.
  - `IncomeDetail.get_entries() -> list[dict] | None`.
  - `IncomeDetail.store_entries(rows: list[dict] | None) -> list[dict] | None`:
    - It doesn't save.
    - Ids work as in `Household.store_members`: a kept id is used once; otherwise the entry takes `next_entry_id`, which then goes up by 1.
    - Every entry carries `id, kind, description, gross, expenses, current_gross, current_expenses`, with null for a missing answer.
    - Amounts are stored as quantized strings.
  - `apps/intake/income.py`: `ENTRY_KINDS = ("business", "rental")`, `ENTRY_KEYS = ("kind", "description", "gross", "expenses", "current_gross", "current_expenses")`, `ENTRY_AMOUNT_KEYS = ("gross", "expenses", "current_gross", "current_expenses")`, `MAX_ENTRIES = 10`.
  - `IncomeEntrySerializer(serializers.Serializer)`:
    - `id`: int, optional, null
    - `kind`: ChoiceField `ENTRY_KINDS`, null
    - `description`: CharField `max_length=200`, null, blank
    - four amounts: `DecimalField(10, 2, min_value=0, allow_null=True)`
  - Both income serializers:
    - `business_and_rental = ListField(child=IncomeEntrySerializer(), allow_null=True, required=False, max_length=MAX_ENTRIES, write_only=True)`, returned through `to_representation` from `get_entries()`.
    - `income_now_different` and `assistance_answer` as `ChoiceField(YES_NO, allow_null=True, required=False)`.
    - Every new amount is declared.
    - `monthly_rental_income` is read-only on both, so a write is ignored.
    - `IncomeInfoSerializer` also returns `income_period_end`, read-only.
  - `serializers._save_income(session, data: dict) -> IncomeInfo` and `serializers._save_spouse_income(session, data: dict) -> SpouseIncome`:
    - They set only the keys sent, and call `store_entries` when `business_and_rental` is sent.
    - A non-null `business_and_rental` sets `monthly_rental_income = None`.
    - They point the session's cached relation at the saved row.
    - `create()` and `update()` both call them.
  - The session PATCH reply lists `business_and_rental` in the order sent, each entry with its id.
  - `test_session_lock.py` `WRITES` gains `"income PATCH"`, with a body carrying `income_info.business_and_rental`.
  - `cmi.py` constants:
    - `LEGACY_RENTAL = "Rental income was saved as one amount. Enter each rental property's gross receipts and operating expenses on the Income step."`
    - `SPOUSE_LEGACY_RENTAL = "Your spouse's rental income was saved as one amount. Enter each rental property's gross receipts and operating expenses on the Income step."`
  - `income_step_gaps`, after the household gaps, in this order:
    - the filer's legacy gap (`monthly_rental_income > 0` and `get_entries() is None`);
    - then "income not entered", only while the legacy gap is not open;
    - under `married_together`, the spouse's legacy gap, then the spouse's income gap, which is not named while the spouse's legacy gap is open.

**Decided here:**

- **A shared serializer base, as the model has one.** `IncomeDetailSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer)` in `serializers.py` declares the 3e2 fields once, and `IncomeInfoSerializer` and `SpouseIncomeSerializer` inherit it:
  - 21 amounts: interest, the ten current, the two assistance amounts and the eight deductions;
  - the two yes/no fields;
  - `business_and_rental`;
  - the three texts as `CharField(max_length=100, allow_blank=True, required=False)`;
  - `monthly_rental_income` as `DecimalField(10, 2, read_only=True)`;
  - and `to_representation` adding `business_and_rental` from `get_entries()`.
- `_INCOME_DETAIL_FIELDS` is a module list naming them for both `Meta.fields`.
- The two serializers would otherwise repeat 27 identical declarations.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`backend/apps/intake/tests/test_income_detail_storage.py`. Its docstring gives the business intent: each new Income step answer is the filer's own, stored encrypted, and nothing starts answered. A missing answer saves for Complete to name, and only a value no answer can have is refused (§4, §6). It uses the `filer` fixture from `test_spouse_and_marriage_storage.py:21-46`, with `_save` and `_raw`.

```python
GIG = {"kind": "business", "description": "Rideshare and delivery driving",
       "gross": "4200.00", "expenses": "830.00", "current_gross": None, "current_expenses": None}
RENTAL = {**GIG, "kind": "rental", "description": "2-flat, 1800 W Example Ave", "gross": "1200.00", "expenses": "300.00"}

def _entries(client, session, rows, person="income_info"):
    response = _save(client, session, {person: {"business_and_rental": rows}})
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.data
    return response.data[person]["business_and_rental"]

@pytest.mark.parametrize("person", ["income_info", "spouse_income"])
def test_a_gig_drivers_entry_saves_with_an_id_and_comes_back_in_the_order_sent(filer, person):
    client, session = filer
    saved = _entries(client, session, [RENTAL, GIG], person)
    assert [e["description"] for e in saved] == [RENTAL["description"], GIG["description"]]
    assert [e["id"] for e in saved] == [1, 2]
    assert saved[1] == {"id": 2, **GIG}

def test_an_entry_id_is_never_reused(filer):
    client, session = filer
    first = _entries(client, session, [GIG, RENTAL])
    kept = _entries(client, session, [first[1]])                 # GIG (id 1) removed
    assert [e["id"] for e in _entries(client, session, [*kept, GIG])] == [2, 3]

def test_a_missing_entry_answer_saves_as_null(filer):
    client, session = filer
    [entry] = _entries(client, session, [{"kind": "business"}])
    assert entry == {"id": 1, "kind": "business", "description": None, "gross": None,
                     "expenses": None, "current_gross": None, "current_expenses": None}

@pytest.mark.parametrize("rows", [
    [GIG] * 11,
    [{**GIG, "kind": "farm"}],
    [{**GIG, "description": "x" * 201}],
    [{**GIG, "gross": "-1.00"}],
    [{**GIG, "expenses": "100000000.00"}],
], ids=["eleventh_entry", "unknown_kind", "long_description", "negative", "too_wide"])
def test_a_value_no_answer_can_have_is_refused(filer, rows):
    client, session = filer
    assert _save(client, session, {"income_info": {"business_and_rental": rows}}).status_code == 400

@pytest.mark.parametrize("field", ["income_now_different", "assistance_answer"])
def test_a_yes_no_answer_takes_only_yes_no_or_null(filer, field):
    client, session = filer
    assert _save(client, session, {"income_info": {field: "maybe"}}).status_code == 400
    assert _save(client, session, {"spouse_income": {field: "maybe"}}).status_code == 400
    assert _save(client, session, {"income_info": {field: None}}).status_code == 200

def test_no_new_question_starts_answered(filer):
    client, session = filer
    income = _save(client, session, {"income_info": {"monthly_gross_wages": "1.00"}}).data["income_info"]
    assert income["business_and_rental"] is None
    assert income["income_now_different"] is None and income["assistance_answer"] is None
    assert all(income[f] is None for f in (*IncomeInfo.CURRENT_FIELDS, *IncomeInfo.DEDUCTION_FIELDS))
    assert income["income_period_end"] is None

def test_saving_the_list_clears_a_legacy_rental_figure_and_a_body_without_it_leaves_it(filer):
    client, session = filer
    IncomeInfo.objects.create(session=session, monthly_rental_income=Decimal("900.00"))
    _save(client, session, {"income_info": {"monthly_gross_wages": "1.00"}})
    assert IncomeInfo.objects.get(session=session).monthly_rental_income == Decimal("900.00")
    _entries(client, session, [])
    assert IncomeInfo.objects.get(session=session).monthly_rental_income is None

@pytest.mark.parametrize("person", ["income_info", "spouse_income"])
def test_a_write_of_the_legacy_rental_figure_is_ignored(filer, person):
    client, session = filer
    response = _save(client, session, {person: {"monthly_rental_income": "500.00", "monthly_tips": "1.00"}})
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.data[person]["monthly_rental_income"] is None

def test_the_income_steps_save_never_changes_a_deduction_and_the_reverse(filer):
    client, session = filer
    _save(client, session, {"income_info": {"payroll_tax": "400.00", "payroll_other_specify": "Parking"}})
    _save(client, session, {"income_info": {"monthly_gross_wages": "3200.00", "business_and_rental": []}})
    row = IncomeInfo.objects.get(session=session)
    assert (row.payroll_tax, row.payroll_other_specify) == (Decimal("400.00"), "Parking")
    _save(client, session, {"income_info": {"payroll_tax": "410.00"}})
    row = IncomeInfo.objects.get(session=session)
    assert (row.monthly_gross_wages, row.get_entries()) == (Decimal("3200.00"), [])

@pytest.mark.parametrize("field", ["payroll_other_specify", "cash_assistance_kind", "noncash_assistance_kind"])
def test_a_text_answer_over_100_characters_is_refused(filer, field):
    client, session = filer
    assert _save(client, session, {"income_info": {field: "x" * 101}}).status_code == 400

# Every encrypted IncomeDetail column (§13 "every migrated or new column is stored as ciphertext").
NEW_AMOUNTS = ("monthly_interest_and_dividends",
               *(f"current_{f}" for f in IncomeInfo.SOURCE_FIELDS),  # the ten CURRENT_FIELDS once Task 2 lands
               "payroll_tax", "payroll_mandatory_retirement", "payroll_voluntary_retirement",
               "payroll_retirement_loan", "payroll_insurance", "payroll_support", "payroll_union_dues",
               "payroll_other", "cash_assistance_amount", "noncash_assistance_value")
NEW_TEXTS = ("payroll_other_specify", "cash_assistance_kind", "noncash_assistance_kind")

@pytest.mark.parametrize("person", ["income_info", "spouse_income"])  # each is also its db_table
@pytest.mark.parametrize("field,value,plain", [
    *[(f, "125.00", "125.00") for f in NEW_AMOUNTS],
    *[(f, "SNAP (food stamps)", "SNAP") for f in NEW_TEXTS],
    ("income_now_different", "yes", None),
    ("assistance_answer", "no", None),
    ("business_and_rental", [GIG], "Rideshare"),
])
def test_every_new_income_column_is_ciphertext(filer, person, field, value, plain):
    client, session = filer
    assert _save(client, session, {person: {field: value}}).status_code == 200
    raw = _raw(f"SELECT {field} FROM {person} WHERE session_id = %s", session)
    assert raw.startswith("gAAAAA") and (plain is None or plain not in raw)
```

```python
def test_a_rental_figure_saved_as_one_amount_is_the_only_gap_named_for_it(filer, completed):
    # Its only income was the legacy figure, so "income not entered" stays unnamed (§4.7).
    client, _ = filer
    IncomeInfo.objects.filter(session=completed).update(monthly_gross_wages=None)
    completed.income_info.refresh_from_db()
    _legacy_rental(completed)
    assert [str(g) for g in income_step_gaps(completed)] == [LEGACY]

def test_a_rental_figure_saved_as_one_amount_refuses_the_means_test_and_the_forms(filer, completed):
    client, _ = filer
    _legacy_rental(completed)                                     # status stays "completed"
    means = client.post(_session_url(completed, "calculate_means_test/"))
    assert (means.status_code, means.data["error"]) == (400, LEGACY)
    forms = client.get("/api/forms/packet/", {"session": completed.pk})
    assert (forms.status_code, forms.data["error"], forms.data["step"]) == (422, LEGACY, "income_info")
    client.patch(_session_url(completed), {"income_info": {"business_and_rental": []}}, format="json")
    assert LEGACY not in [str(g) for g in income_step_gaps(IntakeSession.objects.get(pk=completed.pk))]
```

### Task 3: Business and rental nets count in CMI; the window and the stamp

**Spec:** §4.2 (the sums and the floor), §4.4 (the window, the stamp, the session read), §4.7, §8 ("income not entered" counting entries).

**Files:**

- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/income.py` (Task 2's constants)
- Modify: `backend/apps/eligibility/cmi.py:32-51` (`current_monthly_income`), `:103-114` (`spouse_monthly_income`); add `income_window` and `answered_window`
- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/models.py` (`IncomeInfo.calculate_total_monthly_income`, Task 2's body of `:211-222`)
- Modify: `backend/apps/eligibility/models.py:188-216` (`calculation_details`)
- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/views.py:185-226` (`update_step`)
- Modify: `backend/apps/intake/serializers.py:406-451` (`IntakeSessionSerializer`: `income_window`)
- Create: `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_income_entries.py`, `backend/apps/intake/tests/test_income_window.py`
- Modify: `backend/apps/eligibility/tests/test_cmi.py` and `backend/apps/eligibility/tests/test_household_cmi.py` (new tests appended), `backend/apps/eligibility/tests/test_means_test_calculator.py` (a `calculation_details` test appended)
- Modify: `CLAUDE.md:74` ("Income is stored by source"), `:35` ("Means test units"), the file-map lines `:99` (`apps/intake/models.py`) and `:105` (`apps/eligibility/cmi.py`), plus a new file-map line for `apps/intake/income.py`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Task 2's columns, `get_entries()`, `ENTRY_KINDS`.
- Produces, in `apps/intake/income.py` (pure: rows are taken by duck type, and entries as stored):
  - `kind_sums(entries: list[dict] | None, kind: str, *, current: bool = False) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal]`: the gross sum and the expenses sum, with a blank counting as 0. `current` reads `current_gross`/`current_expenses`.
  - `kind_net(entries, kind, *, current=False) -> Decimal`, which may be below 0.
  - `floored_net(entries, kind, *, current=False) -> Decimal`: `max(Decimal("0.00"), kind_net)`.
  - `has_entry_figures(entries) -> bool`: true when an entry has `gross` or `expenses` not null.
  - `uses_current(row) -> bool`: `row.income_now_different == "yes"`.
  - `schedule_i_field(row, field: str) -> str`: `"current_" + field` when `uses_current(row)`, else `field`.
- Produces, in `cmi.py`:
  - `current_monthly_income(row) -> Decimal`: the nine sources other than Social Security (interest included), plus `floored_net(..., "business")` and `floored_net(..., "rental")` from the averages, rounded half-even to the cent. It raises `IncomeNotSplit` only when none of the ten was given and `not has_entry_figures(entries)`.
  - `spouse_monthly_income` changes the same way: the spouse's gap fires only when none of the ten was given and there are no entry figures.
  - `income_window(on: date) -> tuple[date, date]`, and `answered_window(period_end: date) -> tuple[date, date]` (`income_window(period_end + timedelta(days=1))`).
- Produces elsewhere:

  - `IncomeInfo.calculate_total_monthly_income()` adds the floored business and rental nets of the averages to the ten, Social Security included. It returns None only when nothing was given at all.
  - `calculation_details` gains `business_net`, `rental_net`, `spouse_business_net` and `spouse_rental_net`, the floored nets CMI added, as strings. The spouse's two are `None` unless a spouse's income counts.
  - `update_step` with `current_step == 3` sets `session.income_info.income_period_end = income_window(fill_date())[1]` when the row exists (`save(update_fields=["income_period_end"])`), and creates nothing otherwise.
  - The session read gains `income_window`, read-only: `{"start": "YYYY-MM-DD", "end": "YYYY-MM-DD"}` from `income_window(fill_date())`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`backend/apps/intake/tests/test_income_entries.py` holds the pure tests, with no database. Its docstring gives the business intent: each kind's net is that person's gross sum less their expenses sum. CMI never counts a loss, because the U.S. Trustee's guidance says the net "cannot be a negative number" (owner answer 1, C-12), and a loss never crosses kinds or people (§4.2).

```python
def _e(kind, gross, expenses, current_gross=None, current_expenses=None):
    return {"id": 1, "kind": kind, "description": "x", "gross": gross, "expenses": expenses,
            "current_gross": current_gross, "current_expenses": current_expenses}

def test_a_loss_on_one_entry_nets_against_a_profit_on_another_of_the_same_kind():
    entries = [_e("business", "1000.00", "200.00"), _e("business", "300.00", "600.00")]
    assert kind_sums(entries, "business") == (Decimal("1300.00"), Decimal("800.00"))
    assert floored_net(entries, "business") == Decimal("500.00")

def test_a_kinds_net_below_zero_counts_as_zero_and_never_touches_the_other_kind():
    entries = [_e("business", "100.00", "400.00"), _e("rental", "1200.00", "200.00")]
    assert kind_net(entries, "business") == Decimal("-300.00")
    assert floored_net(entries, "business") == Decimal("0.00")
    assert floored_net(entries, "rental") == Decimal("1000.00")

def test_the_current_figures_have_their_own_net():
    entries = [_e("rental", "1200.00", "200.00", current_gross="500.00", current_expenses="900.00")]
    assert floored_net(entries, "rental", current=True) == Decimal("0.00")
    assert floored_net(entries, "rental") == Decimal("1000.00")

def test_a_blank_amount_counts_as_zero_and_no_list_nets_zero():
    assert kind_sums([_e("business", None, "50.00")], "business") == (Decimal("0"), Decimal("50.00"))
    assert floored_net(None, "business") == Decimal("0.00")
    assert not has_entry_figures([_e("business", None, None)]) and has_entry_figures([_e("rental", "0.00", None)])

def test_schedule_i_uses_the_current_figures_only_while_the_answer_is_yes():
    for answer, field in [("yes", "current_monthly_tips"), ("no", "monthly_tips"), (None, "monthly_tips")]:
        assert schedule_i_field(SimpleNamespace(income_now_different=answer), "monthly_tips") == field
```

Append to `backend/apps/eligibility/tests/test_cmi.py`, using its `filer` fixture (`:35-60`):

```python
GIG_ENTRY = {"kind": "business", "description": "Rideshare and delivery driving",
             "gross": "4200.00", "expenses": "830.00", "current_gross": None, "current_expenses": None}

def _income(session, entries, **amounts):
    row = IncomeInfo(session=session, **amounts)
    row.store_entries(entries)
    row.save()
    return row

def test_a_gig_drivers_cmi_is_his_net(filer):
    _, session = filer
    row = _income(session, [GIG_ENTRY])              # gross 4200.00, expenses 830.00, nothing else typed
    assert current_monthly_income(row) == Decimal("3370.00")   # an Andrés with only business income is not refused

def test_a_business_loss_never_lowers_cmi_the_total_or_is_refused(filer):
    _, session = filer
    row = _income(session, [{"kind": "business", "gross": "500.00", "expenses": "900.00"}],
                  monthly_gross_wages=Decimal("2000.00"))
    assert current_monthly_income(row) == Decimal("2000.00")
    assert row.calculate_total_monthly_income() == 2000.0

def test_a_business_loss_never_lowers_rental_income(filer):
    _, session = filer
    row = _income(session, [{"kind": "business", "gross": "100.00", "expenses": "400.00"},
                            {"kind": "rental", "gross": "1200.00", "expenses": "200.00"}],
                  monthly_gross_wages=Decimal("2000.00"))
    assert current_monthly_income(row) == Decimal("3000.00")

def test_entries_with_no_figures_leave_income_not_entered(filer):
    _, session = filer
    with pytest.raises(IncomeNotSplit):
        current_monthly_income(_income(session, [{"kind": "rental"}]))
```

`backend/apps/intake/tests/test_income_window.py`. Its docstring gives the business intent: 122A-1's six months are the six full calendar months before the filing month ("if you are filing on September 15, the 6-month period would be March 1 through August 31"). The server works them out from the fill date, and Continue on the Income step records the window the filer confirmed (§4.4).

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("on,window", [
    (date(2026, 10, 1), (date(2026, 4, 1), date(2026, 9, 30))),
    (date(2026, 10, 31), (date(2026, 4, 1), date(2026, 9, 30))),
    (date(2027, 1, 15), (date(2026, 7, 1), date(2026, 12, 31))),
    (date(2026, 3, 10), (date(2025, 9, 1), date(2026, 2, 28))),
    (date(2027, 3, 1), (date(2026, 9, 1), date(2027, 2, 28))),
    (date(2028, 3, 31), (date(2027, 9, 1), date(2028, 2, 29))),
], ids=["october_1st", "october_31st", "january", "march_after_february", "across_a_year_end", "leap_year"])
def test_the_window_is_the_six_full_months_before_the_fill_date(on, window):
    assert income_window(on) == window

def test_the_window_answered_is_the_one_whose_last_day_was_stamped():
    assert answered_window(date(2026, 9, 30)) == (date(2026, 4, 1), date(2026, 9, 30))

def test_continue_from_the_income_step_stamps_the_window_and_continue_from_the_next_does_not(filer):
    client, session = filer                                    # _today pinned to 2026-10-16
    IncomeInfo.objects.create(session=session, monthly_gross_wages=Decimal("1.00"))
    client.post(f"/api/intake/sessions/{session.pk}/update_step/", {"current_step": 4}, format="json")
    assert IncomeInfo.objects.get(session=session).income_period_end is None
    client.post(f"/api/intake/sessions/{session.pk}/update_step/", {"current_step": 3}, format="json")
    assert IncomeInfo.objects.get(session=session).income_period_end == date(2026, 9, 30)

def test_an_autosave_of_the_income_step_never_stamps_it(filer):
    client, session = filer
    client.patch(f"/api/intake/sessions/{session.pk}/", {"income_info": {"monthly_tips": "1.00"}}, format="json")
    assert IncomeInfo.objects.get(session=session).income_period_end is None

def test_a_session_with_no_income_row_is_stamped_with_nothing(filer):
    client, session = filer
    response = client.post(f"/api/intake/sessions/{session.pk}/update_step/", {"current_step": 3}, format="json")
    assert response.status_code == 200 and not IncomeInfo.objects.filter(session=session).exists()

def test_the_session_read_names_the_window(filer, monkeypatch):
    client, session = filer
    monkeypatch.setattr("apps.districts.dated_tables._today", lambda: date(2027, 3, 5))
    window = client.get(f"/api/intake/sessions/{session.pk}/").json()["income_window"]
    assert window == {"start": "2026-09-01", "end": "2027-02-28"}
```

```
feat(eligibility): count business and rental nets in CMI; name the window

Each person's business and rental entries add their gross sum less their
expenses sum per kind, never below $0.00, so a loss never lowers CMI or
crosses kinds or people. The server works out the six-month window from
the fill date, the session read names it, and Continue from the Income
step stamps the window the filer confirmed.
```

```python
def _url(session):
    return f"/api/intake/sessions/{session.pk}/"
```

```python
NEW_LINES = {"real_estate_taxes": "210.00", "home_insurance": "15.00", "hoa_dues": "75.00",
             "personal_care": "40.00", "entertainment": "45.00", "charitable_contributions": "50.00",
             "taxes_not_deducted": "200.00", "support_others": "100.00", "other_property_mortgages": "600.00",
             "other_property_taxes": "90.00", "other_property_insurance": "30.00",
             "other_property_upkeep": "25.00", "other_property_hoa_dues": "20.00"}   # sums to 1500.00

def test_the_new_schedule_j_lines_reach_the_total_every_form_prints(filer_session):
    client, session = filer_session
    client.patch(_url(session), {"expense_info": {"rent_or_mortgage": "1000.00", **NEW_LINES}}, format="json")
    assert client.get(_url(session)).json()["expense_info"]["total_monthly_expenses"] == 2500.0
    assert DERIVATIONS["fee_waiver_monthly_expenses"](session) == "2500.00"   # the one function behind J 22a/22c/23b, 106Sum 5, 103B 6

def test_an_empty_expenses_body_stores_26_zero_amounts_and_leaves_line_3_unanswered(filer_session):
    client, session = filer_session
    reply = client.patch(_url(session), {"expense_info": {}}, format="json").json()["expense_info"]
    row = ExpenseInfo.objects.get(session=session)
    assert len(ExpenseInfo.AMOUNT_FIELDS) == 26
    assert all(Decimal(getattr(row, f)) == 0 for f in ExpenseInfo.AMOUNT_FIELDS)
    assert row.expenses_include_others is None and reply["expenses_include_others"] is None
    assert all(reply[f] == "" for f in ExpenseInfo.SPECIFY_FIELDS)

def test_line_3_takes_only_yes_no_or_null(filer_session):
    client, session = filer_session
    assert client.patch(_url(session), {"expense_info": {"expenses_include_others": "maybe"}}, format="json").status_code == 400
    assert client.patch(_url(session), {"expense_info": {"expenses_include_others": "yes"}}, format="json").status_code == 200

@pytest.mark.parametrize("field", ["taxes_specify", "support_others_specify", "other_expenses_specify"])
def test_a_specify_text_over_100_characters_is_refused(filer_session, field):
    client, session = filer_session
    assert client.patch(_url(session), {"expense_info": {field: "x" * 101}}, format="json").status_code == 400

@pytest.mark.parametrize("field,value,plain", [
    *[(name, amount, amount) for name, amount in NEW_LINES.items()],
    *[(name, "IRS installment agreement, 2025 tax", "IRS") for name in ExpenseInfo.SPECIFY_FIELDS],
    ("expenses_include_others", "yes", None),
])
def test_every_new_expense_column_is_ciphertext(filer_session, field, value, plain):
    client, session = filer_session
    client.patch(_url(session), {"expense_info": {field: value}}, format="json")
    with connection.cursor() as cursor:
        cursor.execute(f"SELECT {field} FROM expense_info WHERE session_id = %s", [session.pk])
        raw = cursor.fetchone()[0]
    assert raw.startswith("gAAAAA") and (plain is None or plain not in raw)
```

**Spec:** §11 (the seed's answers other than Schedule J's new amounts, Andrés's business and `current_step`), §13 (the `test_api_readback.py` bodies).

**Files:**

- Modify: `CLAUDE.md:29` ("Seed order")

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Tasks 2–4's columns; `income_window` (`apps.eligibility.cmi`) and `fill_date` (`apps.districts.dated_tables`).
- Produces (the seed, per person and per spouse at home):
  - `monthly_interest_and_dividends = 0`. This is already true since Task 2, through `SOURCE_FIELDS`.
  - `business_and_rental = []`, stored through `store_entries([])`. Andrés gets this too, until Task 14.
  - `income_now_different = "no"`.
  - `assistance_answer = "no"`, except Maria: `"yes"` with `noncash_assistance_kind = "SNAP (food stamps)"` and `noncash_assistance_value = 350`.
  - Deductions:
    - Maria: 5a 400, and 5b–5h 0
    - James: `payroll_tax` 1288, `payroll_union_dues` 45, the rest null
    - Priya: `payroll_tax` 2450, `payroll_voluntary_retirement` 600, `payroll_insurance` 620. Her husband: `payroll_tax` 250
    - DeShawn: `payroll_tax` 450. His wife: `payroll_tax` 115
    - Sarah: `payroll_tax` 250, the rest null
    - Andrés: all null
  - `expenses_include_others`: Priya and DeShawn `"yes"`; Maria, James, Sarah and Andrés `"no"`.
  - `income_period_end = income_window(fill_date())[1]`.
  - The seed stops writing `monthly_rental_income`. Task 2 already did this through `SOURCE_FIELDS`; this task asserts it.
- `test_api_readback.py`'s `_step_bodies` sends the same answers in the Income body (Task 2's keys). Task 8 adds the Take-home pay PATCH, and Task 9 adds a line 3 assertion to `_send_through_the_api`.

**Decided here:**

- The persona dicts carry the deductions inside `"income"` and `"spouse_income"`, so `_create_persona` stores them with the income row.
- `_step_bodies` sends the whole `"income"` dict as today. Task 8 splits the deductions out into the Take-home pay body.
- `"business_and_rental"` sits in the dicts as `[]`.
- `_create_persona` builds each row with `IncomeInfo(session=session, **fields)` (and the same for `SpouseIncome`). It pops `business_and_rental` and calls `row.store_entries(entries)`, sets `row.income_period_end` on the filer's row, then calls `row.save()`. An `EncryptedTextField` given a Python list would store its `str()`.
- Line 3 sits in `"expenses"`, so `ExpenseInfo.objects.create(**data["expenses"])` stores it unchanged.

```python
DEDUCTIONS = {
    "maria": ({"payroll_tax": "400.00", **dict.fromkeys(DEDUCTION_FIELDS[1:], "0.00")}, None),
    "james": ({"payroll_tax": "1288.00", "payroll_union_dues": "45.00"}, None),
    "priya": ({"payroll_tax": "2450.00", "payroll_voluntary_retirement": "600.00", "payroll_insurance": "620.00"},
              {"payroll_tax": "250.00"}),
    "deshawn": ({"payroll_tax": "450.00"}, {"payroll_tax": "115.00"}),

    "andres": ({}, None),
}
LINE_3 = {"maria": "no", "james": "no", "priya": "yes", "deshawn": "yes", "sarah": "no", "andres": "no"}
CMI = {"maria": "3200.00", "james": "5833.00", "priya": "13800.00", "deshawn": "4500.00",
       "sarah": "2000.00", "andres": "3370.00"}

def _deductions(row):
    return {f: getattr(row, f) for f in DEDUCTION_FIELDS if getattr(row, f) is not None}

@pytest.mark.django_db
@pytest.mark.parametrize("persona", list(DEDUCTIONS))
def test_every_seeded_persona_answers_each_new_income_question(persona):
    call_command("loaddata", "ilnd_2025_data", verbosity=0)
    call_command("seed_demo_data", persona=persona, stdout=StringIO())
    session = IntakeSession.objects.get(user__username=f"demo_{persona}")
    filer_deductions, spouse_deductions = DEDUCTIONS[persona]
    rows = [(session.income_info, filer_deductions)]
    if spouse_deductions is not None:
        rows.append((session.spouse_income, spouse_deductions))
    for row, deductions in rows:
        assert row.get_entries() == []
        assert row.income_now_different == "no"
        assert row.monthly_interest_and_dividends == Decimal("0.00")
        assert row.monthly_rental_income is None
        assert _deductions(row) == {k: Decimal(v) for k, v in deductions.items()}  # compares amounts, not spellings
    filer = session.income_info
    if persona == "maria":
        assert (filer.assistance_answer, filer.noncash_assistance_kind, filer.noncash_assistance_value,
                filer.cash_assistance_kind, filer.cash_assistance_amount) == (
                "yes", "SNAP (food stamps)", Decimal("350.00"), "", None)
    else:
        assert all(r.assistance_answer == "no" for r, _ in rows)
    assert session.expense_info.expenses_include_others == LINE_3[persona]
    assert filer.income_period_end == date(2026, 9, 30)      # income_window of the pinned 2026-10-16

@pytest.mark.django_db

    assert means_test.qualifies_for_fee_waiver is (persona in {"maria", "deshawn", "sarah", "andres"})
    forms = packet(session)
    assert ("form_122a2" in forms) is (persona == "priya")
    assert ("form_103b" in forms) is (persona in {"maria", "deshawn", "sarah"})
```
